This framework unifies commuting Pauli families, where sufficient common-eigenspace degeneracy yields polytopes, and subsystem stabilizer codes, where protected logical subsystems supply multiplicity spaces for noncommuting gauge observables. Explicit examples show how increasing the code dimension can make a range shrink, collapse, or become empty, while increasing multiplicity can turn a Bloch sphere into a filled ball. In the subsystem setting, parent-Hamiltonian constructions realize exact detecting codes as degenerate ground spaces, selecting distinguished vectors and continuous paths within these ranges. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. The results provide a structural framework for Knill-Laflamme coefficient geometry and motivate sharper criteria for connected coefficient ranges and interval signature spectra under structured error models.

</details>


### [128] [Operational transformation rates of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02025)
*Giulia Mazzola,Renato Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文放松量子态的iid假设，提出从操作角度与张量幂态不可区分的资源态集合，并用de Finetti型论证证明该集合渐近可由近似iid态表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常用量子态的张量幂描述多个拷贝，但从操作角度看，实验者无法排除未知位置上的不完美或拷贝间相关性，因此iid假设缺乏先验合理性，需要放松该假设。

Method: 考虑一整套与张量幂态在操作上不可区分、因而对实验者而言"看起来像"多拷贝量子态的资源态集合；利用de Finetti型论证分析该操作集合的渐近表示。

Result: 证明该操作集合渐近可由近似iid态表示。

Conclusion: 作为应用，引入渐近变换率的操作类比，如纠缠代价与纠缠蒸馏；并提出这些操作率是否与iid对应量一致的问题。

Abstract: Typically, multiple copies of a quantum state are described by a tensor power of that state. However, from an operational point of view, an experimentalist cannot, in general, rule out imperfections at unknown positions or correlations between copies, so assuming independent and identically distributed (iid) copies is not justified a priori. In the following, we relax the iid assumption by considering a whole set of resource states that are fundamentally indistinguishable from a tensor power state, and in that sense look like many copies of the quantum state to the experimentalist. Using a de Finetti-type argument, we show that this operational set of states can asymptotically be represented by almost-iid states. As an application, we introduce operational analogues of asymptotic transformation rates, such as entanglement cost and distillation. This raises the question of whether these rates coincide with their iid counterparts.

</details>


### [129] [(A Variant of) Clifford Circuit Synthesis is NP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02029)
*Luna Lima Keller,Richard Kueng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最优Clifford电路综合问题，证明了该问题是NP-hard的，与已有的启发式算法（如归约到3-SAT）形成匹配的复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优电路综合在经典与量子硬件设计中都是核心问题。经典版本的复杂度已被充分理解，但量子最优电路综合的复杂度认识还很有限。作者希望明确Clifford电路最优综合的复杂度地位。

Method: 证明分两步：(i) 将3-正则图上的3-边着色问题归约到一个仅使用CZ门的电路综合问题；(ii) 证明额外可用的基本Clifford门（尤其是Hadamard门、相位门和CNOT门）不会进一步降低最优电路深度。

Result: 最优Clifford综合的一个变体是NP-hard的，从而给出了与现有启发式方法相匹配的复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对Clifford电路复杂度的理论理解：Clifford电路的模拟与等价性检验属于P，而判断一个Clifford酉算子是否存在给定深度内的实现是NP完全的。

Abstract: Optimal circuit synthesis is the problem of finding the shortest-depth circuit representation of a given functionality with respect to a pre-specified elementary gate set. This is a central problem in both quantum and classical hardware design. While the classical version is very well understood -- both in terms of heuristics and rigorous hardness assertions -- much less is known about optimal quantum circuit synthesis.
  We focus on optimal Clifford circuit synthesis, for which various heuristics are known, e.g. via reduction to 3-SAT. Our main result supplies a matching hardness result: a variant of optimal Clifford synthesis is NP-hard. The proof proceeds in two parts: (i) reduce 3-edge colorability on 3-regular graphs to a circuit synthesis problem that only involves CZ gates, (ii) prove that the availability of additional elementary Clifford gates -- most notably: Hadamard, phase and CNOT -- cannot lead to further improvements of the optimal circuit depth. Our work sharpens the complexity-theoretic understanding of Clifford circuits: simulation and equivalence checking are in P, whereas deciding whether a Clifford unitary admits an implementation within a prescribed depth is NP-complete.

</details>


### [130] [Sparse Hamiltonian simulation with optimal dependence on the maximum column Euclidean norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02030)
*Zecheng Li,Chunhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种模拟d-稀疏厄米哈密顿量的量子算法，将Low算法中的次多项式开销替换为加性对数精度项，并在特定参数下达到最坏情况下界，同时应用于稀疏量子线性系统求解与黑盒酉算子实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low [STOC 2019] 的稀疏哈密顿量模拟算法存在次多项式（subpolynomial）开销，效率未达最优；此外Berry和Childs [QIC 2012] 提出的黑盒酉算子实现的开问题尚未解决。

Method: 在已知最大列欧几里得范数上界Λ的条件下，构造量子算法模拟d-稀疏厄米哈密顿量H，通过稀疏oracle查询实现，并扩展至稀疏量子线性系统求解和黑盒酉算子实现。

Result: 当tΛ≥1/2时，模拟达到算子范数误差ε需O(tΛ√d+√d log(2/ε))次稀疏oracle查询；当d>1且tΛ≥log(2/ε)时达到最坏情况下界。求解d-稀疏量子线性系统需O(κ√d polylog(κ/ε))次查询；黑盒酉算子实现需O(√d log(2/ε))次查询，常数误差下界最优，任意N×N酉算子需Θ(√N)次查询。

Conclusion: 该算法消除了Low算法的次多项式开销，达到接近最优的查询复杂度，并解决了Berry和Childs关于黑盒酉算子实现的开放问题。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for simulating a $d$-sparse Hermitian Hamiltonian $H$, assuming a known upper bound $Λ$ on its maximum column Euclidean norm $\|H\|_{1\to2}$. For $tΛ\ge1/2$, simulation with operator-norm error $ε$ uses \[
  O\!\left(tΛ\sqrt d+\sqrt d\log(2/ε)\right) \] sparse-oracle queries. This removes the subpolynomial overhead in Low's algorithm [STOC 2019], replacing it with an additive logarithmic precision term. For $d>1$ and $tΛ\ge\log(2/ε)$, the bound matches the worst-case lower bound. A known spectral-norm upper bound may also be used in place of $Λ$. The number of 1- and 2-qubit gates is linear in the query scale, up to oracle costs and polynomial overhead in the input bit lengths and logarithmic precision parameters.
  As applications, we obtain $O(κ\sqrt d\,\mathrm{polylog}(κ/ε))$ queries for solving $d$-sparse quantum linear systems with $\|A\|\le1$ and $\|A^{-1}\|\leκ$, under standard sparse and state-preparation access. We also give a gate-efficient implementation of black-box unitaries with at most $d$ nonzero entries per row and column using $O(\sqrt d\log(2/ε))$ queries, given sparse access to the unitary and its adjoint. At constant error, the query bound is optimal and yields $Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries for arbitrary $N\times N$ unitaries, resolving the open question on black-box unitary implementation posed by Berry and Childs [QIC 2012].

</details>


### [131] [Adaptivity is all you need: Optimal stabilizer learning using just single-copy measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02031)
*L. Bittel,J. Eisert,W. Gong,A. A. Mele,L. Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明自适应单拷贝Clifford测量能以Θ(n)样本量学习任意n量子比特稳定子态，完全消除与非自适应单拷贝测量(Ω(n²))及双拷贝Bell测量(Θ(n))之间的差距，并将结果推广到测试任务与有界T门Clifford电路态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态是量子计算的核心，涉及量子纠错、基准测试与高效经典模拟。然而其可学习性存在显著差距：双拷贝Bell测量需Θ(n)份样本，而非自适应单拷贝测量需Ω(n²)份，即单拷贝测量下单拷贝条件似乎固有地低效，这一差距的成因与能否弥合尚不清楚。

Method: 提出多项式时间的自适应算法，仅使用单拷贝Clifford测量学习任意n量子比特稳定子态；核心机制为自适应测量策略。相同思路用于构造样本最优的单拷贝容忍测试器，并在使用k量子比特量子内存时给出不忠实度ε下的最优测试权衡Θ(n−k+1/ε)。进一步将自适应机制推广至稳定子零化度不超过r的态（含有限T门Clifford电路制备的态），给出O(n2^r)单拷贝测量上界。

Result: 自适应算法从Θ(n)单拷贝Clifford测量中学习任意n量子比特稳定子态，达到与Bell采样相同的最优样本复杂度，且无需多拷贝测量；得到样本最优单拷贝容忍测试器；对k量子比特量子内存情形达到最优测试权衡Θ(n−k+1/ε)；对稳定子零化度≤r的态（包括有界T门数Clifford电路态）可用O(n2^r)单拷贝测量学习。

Conclusion: 自适应性可完全弥合稳定子态学习在单拷贝与双拷贝测量间的样本复杂度差距，单拷贝Clifford测量加上自适应即可达到信息论最优。该框架还统一给出样本最优的测试器与量子内存权衡，并扩展至近稳定子态（有界T门）的高效学习，表明自适应测量在量子态学习中具有核心作用。

Abstract: Stabilizer states are central to quantum computing, underlying quantum error correction, benchmarking, and efficient classical simulation. Yet their learnability exhibits a striking gap: an $n$-qubit stabilizer state can be learned from $Θ(n)$ copies using two-copy Bell measurements, whereas non-adaptive single-copy measurements require $Ω(n^2)$ copies. Here we show that adaptivity completely closes this gap. We give a polynomial-time adaptive algorithm that learns an arbitrary $n$-qubit stabilizer state from $Θ(n)$ single-copy Clifford measurements, matching the optimal sample complexity of Bell sampling without any multi-copy measurements. The same ideas yield a sample-optimal single-copy tolerant tester and, with $k$ qubits of quantum memory, the optimal testing tradeoff $Θ(n-k+1/\varepsilon)$ at infidelity $\varepsilon$. Finally, we show that this adaptive mechanism extends beyond exact stabilizer states: states of stabilizer nullity at most $r$, including states prepared by Clifford circuits with a bounded number of $T$ gates, can be learned using $O(n2^{r})$ single-copy measurements.

</details>


### [132] [Nonlocality without entanglement for multipartite quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02032)
*Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了量子测量层面存在"无纠缠的非局域性"：一对双量子比特乘积测量在联合访问时可完美区分，但在LOCC下不可完美区分，并进一步刻画了自适应策略优势、方向性区分及多体推广与光学实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子非局域性传统上以量子态为研究对象（如无纠缠的非局域性、量子数据隐藏）。作者希望将这一概念迁移到"测量"这一对偶对象上，探究测量集合在全局联合操作与局域操作加经典通信（LOCC）之间的区分能力差异，从而建立测量类比版本，并考察其是否能在更简单的设定中出现、是否具有更丰富的结构层次。

Method: 构造具体的测量集合进行对比分析：给出双量子比特乘积测量对的例子；构造六组双分体qutrit乘积测量以比较自适应与非自适应LOCC协议；分析LOCC区分的方向性（取决于哪一方发起协议）；构造n量子比特乘积测量对，使其全局可完美区分但在任意二分下即使允许双侧任意联合量子操作也无法完美区分；并设计基于量子过程非局域性的量子数据隐藏的光学实现方案。

Result: 1）存在一对双量子比特乘积测量，联合访问时可完美区分，但LOCC下不能完美区分，且比态的情形更简单；2）自适应LOCC协议可严格优于受限的非自适应协议，由六组双分体qutrit乘积测量证明，形成区分策略层级；3）LOCC可区分性本质上是方向性的，依赖发起方；4）构造出n量子比特乘积测量对，全局可完美区分但对任意二分均不可完美区分，即使允许二分两侧任意联合量子操作；5）基于量子过程无纠缠非局域性的量子数据隐藏可在基本光学元件上实现。

Conclusion: 测量层面的"无纠缠的非局域性"确实存在且结构丰富：它出现在极简的双量子比特乘积测量中，并伴随自适应优势、方向性及多体（任意二分不可区分）等层级特征；该现象还可用于量子数据隐藏并通过基础光学器件实现，表明测量非局域性是一类具有实际应用潜力的独立资源。

Abstract: We show that a pair of two-qubit product measurements can be perfectly distinguished when accessed jointly, yet cannot be perfectly distinguished when their local components are distributed between distant parties restricted to local quantum operations and classical communication (LOCC). This establishes a measurement analogue of nonlocality without entanglement that, unlike its state-based counterpart, emerges in a remarkably simple setting. We further uncover a hierarchy of LOCC discrimination strategies: adaptive protocols, in which the probe used at one site may depend on classical information obtained from another, can strictly outperform restricted non-adaptive protocols, as demonstrated by a set of six bipartite qutrit product measurements. We also show that LOCC distinguishability of bipartite measurements can be intrinsically directional, depending on which party initiates the protocol. More generally, we construct pairs of $n$-qubit product measurements that are globally perfectly distinguishable but cannot be perfectly distinguished across any bipartition, even when all parties on either side of the bipartition are allowed arbitrary joint quantum operations. Finally, we show how quantum data hiding based on quantum-process nonlocality without entanglement can be realized using elementary optical components.

</details>


### [133] [Complexity and Applications of Nearest Stabilizer Product State Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02037)
*Daniel Grier,Hakop Pashayan,Luke Schaeffer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在稳定子乘积态上的最优化问题（近稳定子乘积态问题），给出其完整复杂度分类：经Clifford群对称性约化后共有9类S，除两个最简单情形外均为NP完全，并展示了该问题在经典模拟、纠缠度量与低秩矩阵完成中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找给定稳定子态在单量子比特稳定子态乘积集合上的最大保真度（或重叠）是一个自然的问题，但其复杂度边界尚不清楚。该问题的解在多个场景中有潜在应用价值，因此有必要系统刻画其计算复杂度。

Method: 定义并研究“近稳定子乘积态问题”：给定n量子比特稳定子态|ψ⟩与单量子比特稳定子态集合S，最大化|⟨ψ|φ1,…,φn⟩|²，其中每个|φi⟩∈S。通过改变S获得不同实例；利用Clifford群对称性对S进行分类，并在约化后识别出9个不同的集合S；随后对各类S下的问题做复杂度归约与分类证明。

Result: 经Clifford群对称性约化后共有9个不同的可能集合S；除其中两个最简单的情况外，其余均为NP完全。同时，问题解在不同设定下可带来：某些经典模拟算法的更紧运行时间上界；纠缠度量；以及低秩矩阵完成的复杂度相关结果。

Conclusion: 近稳定子乘积态问题具有高度丰富的复杂度结构：其9类对称性不等价的S中大多数为NP完全，仅两种最简单情形可高效处理；该统一框架将经典模拟、纠缠度量与低秩矩阵完成等主题联系起来。

Abstract: Consider the following optimization problem over stabilizer product states: given an $n$-qubit stabilizer state $\left|ψ\right\rangle$ and a set of single-qubit stabilizer states $S$, maximize $\left|\langle ψ| φ_1, \ldots, φ_n \rangle\right|^2$ over single-qubit stabilizer states $\left|φ_i\right\rangle \in S$. By varying the set $S$, we show that solutions to this problem can be useful in a variety of settings: tighter runtime bounds for certain classical simulation algorithms; measures of entanglement; and the complexity of low-rank matrix completion. Moreover, we give a complete complexity classification of this nearest stabilizer product state problem. After accounting for the symmetries in the Clifford group, there are $9$ distinct possible sets $S$, and we show that all but the two simplest of these are $\NP$-complete.

</details>


### [134] [Fermionic Gaussianity can be tested with mode-independent sample-complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02050)
*Maxwell West,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的通用技术，用于上界两副本测试对远离目标态的接受概率，并将其应用于费米子高斯态与Slater行列式态的判定问题，证明Bell采样达到最优样本复杂度Θ(ε⁻²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知量子态是否属于或远离某态族是量子信息论的自然问题。此类问题通常存在两副本测试，对属于目标态的输入总能接受，但对远离态族的输入，其上界接受概率难以分析，从而难以确定判定问题的样本复杂度。

Method: 发展了一种新的通用技术，用于上界一般态族下两副本测试的接受概率，进而上界判定问题本身的样本复杂度。该框架被应用于费米子高斯态判定：分析常用的Bell采样过程，并证明其最优性，甚至优于允许任意集体与自适应测量的协议。

Result: 判定未知纯n模态是费米子高斯态还是与所有高斯态在迹距离上至少ε远，可以用最优副本数Θ(ε⁻²)完成。Bell采样在该问题上被证明是最优的。类似地，Slater行列式态族的判定问题也能以与模数无关的样本复杂度求解。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个简单而通用的上界接受概率的技术，统一处理态族判定问题的样本复杂度，并以费米子高斯态和Slater行列式态为例，证明Bell采样为最优协议，且可推广至更广的态族。

Abstract: Deciding whether an unknown quantum state either belongs to, or is far from, a given family of states is a natural question of quantum information theory. In many such cases, there is a natural 2-copy test which always accepts whenever the state indeed belongs to the target family; it is often much more difficult, however, to bound the probability of acceptance for states that are far from the family. Here we develop a simple new technique for upper bounding this probability for general families, and therefore upper bounding the sample-complexity of the decision problem itself. As a particularly striking example of this framework, we show that deciding whether an unknown pure $n$-mode state is fermionic Gaussian, or at least $\varepsilon$-far in trace distance from all Gaussian states, can be accomplished using the optimal number $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ of copies. Remarkably, our analysis applies to the well-known Bell sampling procedure for testing fermionic Gaussianity, which we therefore show to be optimal, even compared to protocols making arbitrary collective and adaptive measurements. As a second example, we show that the analogous decision problem for the family of Slater determinant states can also be solved with mode-independent sample-complexity.

</details>


### [135] [Sequential Capacity of Quantum Processes with Finite Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02068)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"顺序响应容量"以刻画固定内存储的量子器件在长时运行中的响应复杂度，并给出了容量与运行长度、概率分辨率之间的紧致标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子器件在固定内存下长时间运行时响应能有多复杂，是一个既有理论意义又有实际价值的问题，需要一种量化度量来区分量子与经典过程的能力差异。

Method: 将复杂度量化为"顺序响应容量"：统计在给定响应概率间隙下，用全新运行的自适应测试阶段能继续区分可能过程的最大阶段数。分析中构造了单可见量子比特上的含时相位旋转方案（无额外内存储），并在存储的经典标签选取相位序列、已知独立Pauli噪声情形下进行容量界推导。

Result: 固定系统与内存尺寸下得到容量与运行长度、概率分辨率的紧致律：固定分辨率时容量约以K log K增长（K为每次运行的步数），构造方案响应概率恰为0或1；而固定基测量的经典随机过程容量仅为线性。对已知独立Pauli噪声，在理想控制与弱残余相位噪声下证明固定小概率间隙时上下界匹配，并识别出残余相位翻转概率的倒数为限制对数增益的相干时间尺度。

Conclusion: 量子器件可借助相干相位旋转获得超越经典的对数级响应容量增益，但其可持续性受残余相位噪声对应的相干时间尺度限制。

Abstract: How complex can the responses of a quantum device become as it runs longer with a fixed internal memory? We quantify this complexity through sequential response capacity: how many adaptive testing stages, each using a fresh run, can continue to separate possible processes by a prescribed gap in response probabilities. For fixed system and memory sizes, we establish a tight law relating this capacity to run length and probability resolution. At fixed resolution, the capacity grows on the order of $K\log K$, where $K$ is the number of time steps in each run. Our construction attains this growth using time-dependent phase rotations on a single visible qubit with no additional internal memory; its tests give response probabilities exactly zero or one. Under the same tests, classical stochastic processes that measure in a fixed basis at every step have only linear capacity at fixed sizes and resolution. For phase sequences selected by a stored classical label, we then quantify how known independent Pauli noise changes this logarithmic enhancement. With ideal controls and weak residual phase noise after correction, we prove matching capacity bounds at a fixed small probability gap. These bounds identify the inverse residual phase-flip probability as the coherence timescale that limits the extra logarithmic growth.

</details>


### [136] [A computational phase diagram for the transverse field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02079)
*Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究横场Ising模型的配分函数与Gibbs态观测量的经典可近似性，给出了由谱宽与温度/横场决定的复杂度相变阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横场Ising模型是量子多体物理的核心模型，其配分函数与Gibbs态性质在经典与量子复杂度理论中具有基础地位。作者希望刻画该模型在何种参数区间内可被高效经典算法近似，以及从何种阈值起问题变得计算困难，从而给出经典-量子可解性的精确边界。

Method: 针对Hamiltonian H = ½∑J_ij Z_iZ_j + ∑h_i^z Z_i + η∑X_i，引入J的谱宽Δ(J)=λ_max(J)−λ_min(J)。在条件Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η ≤ 1下，构造随机化经典算法近似配分函数Z(β)=Tr(e^{-βH})，并进一步近似Gibbs态ρ_β上Pauli串观测量的期望；在Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η > 1时，通过归约论证其近似困难性。

Result: 当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η ≤ 1时，存在随机化经典算法在关于n、β、模型参数及ε^{-1}的多项式时间内，以相对误差ε近似Z(β)；同一条件下可高效近似Gibbs态Pauli串观测量的期望，达到任意小加性误差，且当观测量在X基下对角（即P∈{I,X}^{⊗n}）时可达到任意小相对误差。当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η > 1时，在exp(o(n))乘性因子内近似Z(β)是NP-hard的，因而不太可能存在高效经典或量子算法（在标准复杂度假设下）。

Conclusion: 横场Ising模型的经典可近似性存在由Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η=1刻画的尖锐阈值：阈值以下配分函数与特定Gibbs态观测量可被高效随机化经典算法近似，阈值以上近似问题NP-hard，从而界定了经典与量子计算可解性的边界。

Abstract: We study the transverse field Ising model, defined by the Hamiltonian $H =\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i, j\in [n]} J_{ij} Z_i Z_j +\sum_{i=1}^n h_i^z Z_i + η\sum_{i} X_i$ where $J $ is the symmetric interaction matrix, and $η$ is the transverse field strength. Let $Δ(J)=λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)$ be the spectral width of $J.$ When the inverse temperature $β\geq0$ satisfies $Δ(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq1$, we give a randomized classical algorithm that approximates the partition function $Z(β)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})$ to a given relative error $ε\in(0,1)$ in time polynomial in $n$, $β$, the model parameters, and $ε^{-1}$. When $ Δ(J) \cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η > 1 ,$ we show that approximating $ Z(β)$ within an $\exp(o(n))$-multiplicative factor is $\textbf{NP}$-hard, and thus unlikely to admit an efficient classical or quantum algorithms under standard complexity theoretic assumptions. Furthermore, in the regime $Δ(J)\cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq 1,$ we provide an efficient randomized classical algorithm that approximates Pauli string observables of the Gibbs state $ ρ_β= \frac{e^{-βH}}{\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})}$ within an arbitrarily small additive error. In the special case when the observable is also diagonal in the $X$-basis, i.e. $P \in \{I, X\}^{\otimes n}$, the algorithm further achieves arbitrarily small relative error.

</details>


### [137] [Stationarity as a One-Mode Constraint on Quantum Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02090)
*Itai Panas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文论证定态量子演化在能量标量意义上的"单一性"：在正则化Hartree–Fock (reg-HF) 框架下，高斯HF表示诱导的相对坐标高斯测度同时取最小Fisher信息、最大Shannon微分熵并满足Stam等号条件；通过Coulomb算子的高斯尺度分解、virial完备化与"单模排除公理"，将端点标量关联响应约束为单模，从而在氦原子上验证了无参数保留Coulomb载流子 $-δ/e$ 与有限-$Z$插入 $8δ/9$ 能复现He-like系数与能量，并区分微观谱复杂度与单模能量充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定态量子态虽具有单一动力学相位因子与单一标量能量，但其内部响应可跨越庞大Hilbert空间。作者关注的并非绝对相位的大小或可观测性，而是定态时间演化的"单一性"如何在正则化Hartree–Fock描述中得到刻画，并试图解释为何复杂的微观谱结构仍可对应单模标量能量。

Method: 采用正则化Hartree–Fock (reg-HF) 框架：在任一单模约化之前，先建立与定态平均场的联系；由高斯HF表示诱导的原积相对坐标高斯测度固定一个协方差，在该协方差处该测度同时为最小Fisher信息分布、最大Shannon微分熵分布及Stam界的等号情形。引入Coulomb算子的兼容高斯尺度分辨率，用virial完备化把正则化配对位移转为定态兼容响应。定态性导出对端点标量关联响应的单模约束，核心假设为"单模排除公理"：将与保留配对模正交的响应分量所携带的协方差从端点标量能量性质中排除，但不断言无限制响应是一维的。以氦为最小非平凡检验对象。

Result: 无参数的保留Coulomb载流子 $-δ/e$ 与有限-$Z$插入 $8δ/9$ 能密切复现独立的He-like系数与能量；显式Bethe–Goldstone计算给出宽广的非单分母返回谱，而该谱的标量能量却可被精确地矩压缩（moment-compressible）。

Conclusion: 结果将微观谱复杂度与单模能量充分性区分开来：即便返回谱呈现多分母的复杂结构，其标量能量仍可完全由单模响应载体描述，支持reg-HF下单模排除公理对定态标量能量的充分性。

Abstract: A stationary quantum state has one dynamical phase factor and one scalar energy even when its internal response spans a large Hilbert space. Our argument concerns not the value or observability of an absolute phase, but the one-ness of stationary time evolution. In regularized Hartree--Fock (reg-HF), the connection to the stationary mean field is established before any one-mode reduction: the primitive-product relative-coordinate Gaussian measure induced by the Gaussian HF representation fixes a covariance and, at that covariance, is simultaneously the minimum-Fisher-information and maximum-Shannon-differential-entropy distribution and the equality case of the Stam bound. The Coulomb operator admits a compatible Gaussian scale resolution, and virial completion converts the resulting regularized pair displacement into a stationary-compatible response. Stationarity motivates a one-mode constraint on the terminal scalar correlation response. The decisive reg-HF hypothesis is a one-mode exclusion axiom: covariance carried by response components orthogonal to the retained pair mode is excluded from the terminal scalar energy property, without asserting that the unrestricted response is one dimensional. Helium provides the minimal nontrivial test. The parameter-free retained Coulomb carrier $-δ/e$ and finite-$Z$ insertion $8δ/9$ closely reproduce independent He-like coefficients and energies, while an explicit Bethe--Goldstone calculation displays a broad non-single-denominator return spectrum even though its scalar energy is exactly moment-compressible. The result separates microscopic spectral complexity from one-mode energetic sufficiency.

</details>


### [138] [One-Way Quantum Symmetric Private Information Retrieval Protocol From A Single Database Server Using NISQ Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02093)
*Xiang Zou,H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于NISQ设备（无长期量子存储、逐到达测量）的对称私有信息检索（SPIR）方案，实现信息论安全：服务器单向量子发送BB84态，用户无歧义分辨后经随机分块置换与哈希奇偶校验，证明方案在单光子及小多光子项下满足窃听者保密性、数据库隐私与用户隐私，且只需BB84设备加一个被动线性光学测量。对于10^4比特数据库与10^-6数据库隐私，约千比特块在容忍噪声信道下足够。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单服务器SPIR对拥有无限制量子能力的参与方在信息论上不可能。已有bounded-和noisy-quantum-storage模型依赖于"有限但大"的量子存储限制，而作者希望采用更严格、更贴近现实硬件的限制：通信双方仅有NISQ设备，无长期量子存储并逐到达测量，而窃听者仍可无限制强大。目标是在这种更强记忆限制下实现具有信息论安全的SPIR，同时尽量贴近BB84实验装备，并量化有限密钥与认证开销。

Method: 服务器单向发送BB84态；用户对每个到达量子比特做无歧义态分辨（USD），只能确定随机子集比特，并配备仅增加一个辅助模的被动线性光学测量。随后双方对位置做联合随机置换并划分为块；用户宣布其私选块的结论性位置，服务器在每个块中对这些位置做哈希并取随机奇偶校验。通过置换使同一位置集合匹配两个块的概率指数小，从而保障隐私。作者给出：对窃听者的保密性证明、通过显式尺寸规则实现数据库隐私、以及对诚实制备规定BB84态并如实宣布的服务器实现用户隐私；对单光子精确成立，对容忍噪声信道扩展至小多光子项。数据库隐私进一步扩展到诱骗态弱相干脉冲，并用有限密钥量化认证任意无记忆用户与承诺置换所需的额外辅助传输。

Result: 证明方案在单光子情形下对窃听者保密、对服务器保障数据库隐私（显式尺寸规则）与用户隐私，并对容忍噪声信道扩展到小多光子项。对10^4比特数据库、数据库隐私10^-6，采用规定测量的用户约需千量级量子比特的块；认证任意无记忆用户与承诺置换需要更大辅助传输（以有限密钥量化）。数据库隐私可延伸至诱骗态弱相干脉冲，且模拟显示所给界近乎紧致。

Conclusion: 在仅有NISQ设备、无长期量子存储且逐到达测量的更严格记忆限制下，单服务器信息论安全SPIR成为可能；方案仅需BB84设备加一个被动线性光学测量与一个辅助模，并明确了块规模、有限密钥开销及噪声信道下的可行性，为实用化量子SPIR提供了贴近现有BB84硬件的路径。

Abstract: Symmetric private information retrieval (SPIR) lets an inquirer obtain one specific bit only, without leaking any information about its index to the database owner. With a single server, this is impossible for parties with unrestricted quantum power. We show that it becomes possible, with information-theoretic security, when the server and the user hold only noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices without long-term quantum memory and measure each qubit on arrival, while the eavesdropper remains all-powerful; this is a stricter form of the memory restriction of the bounded- and noisy-quantum-storage models. The server sends BB84 states one way; the user discriminates them unambiguously and so knows a random subset of the bits. After a joint random permutation into blocks, the user announces the conclusive locations of one privately chosen block, and the server hashes and takes a random parity of these locations in every block; the permutation makes it exponentially unlikely that one set of locations fits two blocks. We prove secrecy against the eavesdropper, database privacy through an explicit size rule for the announced set, and user privacy against a server who prepares the prescribed BB84 states and announces truthfully, exactly for single photons and up to a small multi-photon term over a tolerated noise channel. The only addition to BB84 equipment is a passive linear-optical measurement with one ancillary mode. For a $10^{4}$-bit database and database privacy $10^{-6}$, blocks of about a thousand qubits suffice over a tolerated noise channel for a user who applies the prescribed measurement; certifying an arbitrary memoryless user and committing to the permutation need larger auxiliary transmissions, which we quantify with finite keys. Database privacy extends to decoy-state weak coherent pulses, and simulations show that the bound is nearly tight.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum state preparation for weighted d-DNNF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02094)
*Steef Hegeman,Joon Hyung Lee,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于由加权 d-DNNF 描述的量子态，可在（至多相差复数运算的）线性时间内构造出制备该量子态的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备问题要求给定量子态描述后高效生成计算该态的量子电路，其核心难点在于态描述的结构与电路构造复杂度之间的关系。现有方法多针对特定表示（如张量网络、决策图）缺乏对加权 d-DNNF 这一兼具确定性与可分解性的通用电路类的系统处理。

Method: 将量子态表示为由加权 d-DNNF（deterministic 确定性、decomposable 可分解）的伪布尔电路，并在此结构上设计构造算法，将态制备归约为沿电路结构的线性遍历，逐节点生成对应的量子门操作。

Result: 对加权 d-DNNF 描述的量子态，可在至多相差复数运算开销的线性时间内获得计算该态的量子电路。

Conclusion: 加权 d-DNNF 是支持高效量子态制备的一类表达结构，态制备复杂度相对描述规模为线性（忽略复数运算代价），为利用知识编译表示进行量子电路生成提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The quantum state preparation problem is to, given a description of a quantum state, efficiently generate a quantum circuit computing the state. We show that for quantum states described by weighted d-DNNF (deterministic, decomposable pseudo-Boolean circuits) a quantum circuit computing the state can be obtained in linear time up to complex arithmetic.

</details>


### [140] [An exponential separation between entanglement-assisted and unassisted one-way quantum communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02099)
*Ryan Anselm,Srijita Kundu,Olivier Lalonde,Ashwin Nayak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了量子通信复杂度中长期悬而未决的问题：首次给出全布尔函数在单向模型中因纠缠存在与否而产生的指数级分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信复杂度中长期存在一个问题：是否存在某类任务在存在纠缠时仅需少量通信，而在无纠缠时却需要大量量子通信？此前仅在关系型问题以及同时消息传递模型下的部分函数中已知此类分离，而对于全布尔函数是否存在这种分离尚未解决。

Method: 作者构造了一族全布尔函数 f_n: {0,1}^n × {0,1}^n → {0,1}，该函数是子群成员问题（subgroup membership problem）的特例，并研究其在单向通信模型下的通信复杂度。

Result: 在单向（one-way）设置下实现了指数级分离：该全布尔函数在有预先纠缠时可用 O(log n) 比特经典通信计算，但在无纠缠时需要 Ω(n^{1/3}) 量子比特的单向量子通信。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为全布尔函数给出了纠缠存在与否导致通信复杂度指数分离的实例，解决了量子通信复杂度中的一个长期开放问题；所用函数为子群成员问题的特例。

Abstract: A longstanding question in quantum communication complexity is whether some task can be accomplished with a small amount of communication in the presence of entanglement, yet require much more quantum communication in the absence of entanglement. Separations of this nature were previously known for relational problems and, in the simultaneous message passing model, for partial functions. But it has remained unresolved whether any such separation exists for a total Boolean function. We resolve this question with an exponential separation in the one-way setting: we exhibit a family of total Boolean functions $f_n\colon \{0,1\}^n \times \{0,1\}^n \to \{0,1\}$ that can be computed with $O(\log n)$ bits of one-way classical communication given prior entanglement, but that require $Ω(n^{1/3})$ qubits of one-way quantum communication without entanglement. Our function is a special case of the subgroup membership problem, first studied in the communication setting by Aaronson, Le Gall, Russell, and Tani.

</details>


### [141] [On the pseudorandomness of simple quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02100)
*Jesko Dujmovic,Jonas Haferkamp,Alexander Poremba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Gowers关于局部随机可逆操作能否产生全局伪随机的猜想在量子幺正情形下的类比，给出两类反例，表明酉设计（统计矩匹配）一般不蕴含伪随机性，并据此提出关于简单量子过程中伪随机性如何涌现的新猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gowers（1996）猜想反复复合局部随机可逆操作可产生与随机置换不可区分的全局置换，该问题长期未解。作者希望在幺正量子类比上取得新进展，并厘清“统计矩匹配（酉设计）”与“伪随机性（不可区分性）”之间的关系，同时回应HMMR（2004）置换猜想的量子类比。

Method: 构造可高效采样的分布族并进行可区分性分析：(1) 对每个固定t，给出一、二量子比特门上的分布族{ν_n}，经T=O_t(n^2 log^2 n)步独立复合后得到n量子比特近似酉t-设计（误差可忽略，exp(-Ω(log^2 n))），但存在高效量子算法仅用O_t(log^2 n)次查询即可区分其与随机；(2) 构造新的反例，在多項式有界阶矩处给出更强的酉设计与伪随机酉之间的分离，但该反例依赖高度结构化的系综，而非简单局部行走。

Result: 第一，否定HMMR置换猜想的幺正类比：即使最简单的量子过程也能在统计上模拟t-设计，却仍可被高效量子算法区分于随机。第二，在多项式有界阶矩上得到更强的酉设计与伪随机酉分离；但该反例需高度结构化系综。因此，仅凭酉设计不足以刻画伪随机性，且用酉设计建模黑洞物理中的信息扰乱需谨慎。

Conclusion: 统计矩匹配（酉设计）一般不蕴含伪随机性，甚至简单局部随机过程也可在矩匹配的同时保持可被高效检测的结构。作者据此提出关于简单量子过程（如随机量子电路）中伪随机性如何合理涌现的新猜想，为后续研究提供方向。

Abstract: Can simple processes appear highly complex? Gowers (Comb. Prob. Comp. '96) conjectured that repeatedly composing local random reversible operations can yield global permutations that are indistinguishable from random. In this work, we study the unitary quantum analog of this question, in an attempt to make new progress on this longstanding conjecture.
  Our first result shows that statistical moment matching in the form of unitary designs does not generically lead to pseudorandomness---even for the simplest quantum processes: for every fixed $t$, we give an efficiently samplable family $\{ν_n\}_n$ of distributions on one- and two-qubit gates such that, after $T=O_t(n^2\log^2 n)$ independent steps, the resulting $n$-qubit ensemble is an approximate unitary $t$-design with negligible error $\exp(-Ω(\log^2 n))$, yet an efficient quantum algorithm distinguishes it from random using only $O_t(\log^2 n)$ queries. This refutes the unitary analog of the Hoory--Magen--Myers--Rackoff conjecture (ICALP '04) for permutations.
  Our second result is a stronger separation between unitary designs and pseudorandom unitaries at polynomially bounded moments; our counterexample, however, requires highly structured ensembles, in contrast with the simple local walks from before. This suggests caution when using unitary designs to model information scrambling in black-hole physics, as even maximally scrambled systems can exhibit structure which is accessible to efficient experiments. Motivated by these findings, we then propose new conjectures for how pseudorandomness can plausibly emerge within simple quantum processes, such as random quantum circuits.

</details>


### [142] [Time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02101)
*Fangqi Dong,Alex Lombardi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在随机预言机模型下证明了破解量子密码学的近最优时间-空间下界：具有S个量子比特非均匀建议的T次查询敌手从n量子比特二进制相位态中恢复随机密钥的概率至多为O((T²+√(ST))/N)，显著优于后量子单向函数的已知界O((T²+ST)/N)，展示了量子密码学在预处理攻击下的新优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子密码学相对于经典密码学在抵抗预处理攻击方面的优势，特别是探索量子通信能否用更少的资源实现更高的安全性，并与后量子单向函数的已知下界进行对比。

Method: 将最优预处理攻击表示为随机矩阵的算子范数，通过迹矩方法在随机预言机上期望界值；利用压缩预言机（Zhandry, Crypto 2019）对迹矩进行自然解释和分析；该方法可视为对Liu（Eurocrypt 2023）方法的简化和推广。

Result: 证明了O((T²+√(ST))/N)的近乎最优时间-空间下界；改进了Liu对QROM中后量子PRG的分析，达到O(T²/N+√(ST/N))的区分优势界；将LMW24的单次查询下界扩展到可进行任意函数查询及多项式次自适应随机预言机查询的敌手；证明了随机二进制相位态对空间S区分器的伪随机性紧界O(√S/N)。

Conclusion: n个量子比特的通信即可在空间达N²的预处理攻击下保持安全（而经典情形需N），揭示了量子密码学的新优势；所提方法统一并推广了多个已有结果，并给出了更紧的界。

Abstract: We prove near-optimal time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography in the random oracle model. Specifically, we show that a $T$-query adversary with $S$ qubits of non-uniform advice can recover a random key $k$ from the $n$-qubit binary phase state $|ψ_k\rangle \propto \sum_{x} R(k,x) |x\rangle$ with probability at most $O(\frac{T^2 + \sqrt{ST}}{N})$ for $N=2^n$. In contrast, the best known bound for post-quantum one-way functions is $O(\frac{T^2 + ST}{N})$, with a trivial attack at $S = N$. This demonstrates a new advantage of quantum cryptography over classical cryptography: $n$ qubits of communication suffice for security against preprocessing attacks with space up to $N^2$ rather than $N$. Our methodology is simple: express the optimal preprocessing attack as the operator norm of a random matrix, and bound this value in expectation over the random oracle via the trace-moment method. These trace moments have a natural interpretation using compressed oracles [Zhandry, Crypto 2019], which we then analyze. This can be viewed as a simplification and generalization of the approach of Liu [Eurocrypt 2023] for proving time-space tradeoffs for breaking post-quantum cryptography. We also prove the following results: (1) We tighten Liu's analysis of post-quantum PRGs in QROM, achieving a distinguishing advantage bound of $O(\frac{T^2}N + \sqrt{\frac{ST}N})$. (2) For unitary synthesis, we extend the one-query lower bound of Lombardi-Ma-Wright [STOC 2024] to hold against adversaries that can make one arbitrary function query along with polynomially many (adaptive) queries to the random oracle, either before or after the function query. This also interprets the original LMW24 result in terms of compressed oracles. (3) Finally, we prove a tight $O(\frac{\sqrt{S}}N)$ bound for the pseudorandomness of random binary phase states against space $S$ distinguishers.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Advantage for Two-Party Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02113)
*Daniel Alabi,Emil T. Khabiboulline*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出信息论安全的量子双方汉明距离协议，在Klauck诚实非抢占消息保持模型下实现O(1)期望误差与O(n)通信量，突破经典纯差分隐私的Ω(√n)下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论协议在纯差分隐私下需Ω(√n)误差、强近似差分隐私下需Ω(√n/log n)误差，O(1)误差仅能在计算假设下实现。作者希望探究量子通信能否在信息论安全下达到O(1)误差。

Method: 在Klauck诚实、非抢占、消息保持模型中构造O(n)通信量子协议，采用"受保护相干往返"与等Gram刚性原理，防止诚实方保留依赖输入的互补信息；通过精确的曲棍球杆散度计算分析近似(ε,δ) QDP。

Result: 纯ε QDP下期望误差至多2/sinh ε + γ（任意γ>0）；近似(ε,δ) QDP下误差更小，且当δ=o(1/n)时保持O(1)与Ω(√n/log n)的分离。协议区分于较弱的预设有道隐私（后者已有经典精确实现）与完全抗保留安全（后者仍可被测量-中止攻击）。

Conclusion: 量子通信可在信息论意义下实现O(1)误差，达到经典仅靠计算假设才能获得的精度。保持非正交量子消息本身构成一种隐私资源。

Abstract: We introduce information-theoretically private quantum protocols for two-party Hamming distance when both parties must output the same estimate. Classically, for input length $n$, information-theoretic protocols require $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ error under pure differential privacy and $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ error under strong approximate differential privacy, whereas computational security permits $O(1)$ error. In Klauck's honest, nonpreemptive, message-preserving model, we give an $O(n)$-communication quantum protocol with pure $\varepsilon$ quantum differential privacy (QDP) and expected error at most $\frac{2}{\sinh \varepsilon}+γ$, for every $γ>0$. For approximate $(\varepsilon, δ)$ QDP, an exact hockey-stick divergence calculation yields strictly smaller error, while preserving the $O(1)$-versus-$Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ separation for $δ=o(1/n)$. Thus, quantum communication achieves $O(1)$ information-theoretic error, matching the accuracy available classically only under computational assumptions.
  The main construction uses a guarded coherent round trip and an equal-Gram rigidity principle that prevents an honest player from retaining input-dependent complementary information. We separate this model from weaker prescribed-channel privacy, which already admits an exact classical realization, and from fully retention-robust security, against which measurement-and-abort attacks remain possible. Therefore, we identify preservation of non-orthogonal quantum messages as a resource for privacy.

</details>


### [144] [Spiking neural networks for streaming qubit readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02129)
*Barry M. Dillon,Aqib Javed,Jim Harkin,Patryk Dabkowski,Benjamin Lienhard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用脉冲神经网络（SNN）判别器实现超导量子比特读出，通过分块处理测量窗口实现流式、时间分辨的状态估计，精度超过匹配滤波并接近全轨迹ANN，配合量化感知训练和hls4ml综合可在FPGA上实现低延迟实时推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器的反馈、校准与纠错需要快速准确的量子比特态判别。频率复用读出本质上是多变量问题，测量轨迹中包含串扰、弛豫事件及其他瞬态非理想因素，传统匹配滤波无法完全刻画，因此需要更强的判别方法。

Method: 引入脉冲神经网络（SNN）作为超导量子比特读出判别器：将测量窗口划分为连续时间块（time chunks），网络利用时间结构并随数据到达逐步更新分类分数，而非等到读出窗口结束。结合量化感知训练（quantisation-aware training）与hls4ml综合，将模型部署到FPGA。

Result: SNN性能优于匹配滤波判别，精度接近全轨迹人工神经网络（ANN）；其核心优势在于提供随读出信号采集而演化的流式、时间分辨量子态估计。在FPGA上，每次推断更新可在下一个读出数据块到达前完成。

Conclusion: SNN为FPGA硬件上低延迟、实时量子比特读出提供了有前景的途径，并对时间关键的量子控制与科学推断应用具有更广泛意义。

Abstract: Fast and accurate qubit-state assignment is essential for feedback, calibration, and error correction in quantum processors. In superconducting platforms, frequency-multiplexed readout makes this task intrinsically multivariate as measured traces can encode crosstalk, qubit-state relaxation events, and other transient nonidealities that are not fully captured by conventional matched filtering. Here, we introduce spiking neural network (SNN) discriminators for superconducting qubit readout. By processing the measurement window in successive time chunks, the networks exploit temporal structure and update classification scores as data arrive, rather than waiting until the end of the readout window. The spiking networks outperform matched-filter discrimination and approach the accuracy of a full-trace artificial neural network. Beyond reaching the performance of artificial neural networks, the key advantage of SNNs is that they provide a streaming, time-resolved estimate of the qubit state that evolves as the readout signal is acquired. Using quantisation-aware training and hls4ml synthesis, we further demonstrate that each FPGA inference update can be completed before the next readout chunk arrives. These results establish spiking neural networks as a promising route to low-latency, real-time qubit readout on FPGA hardware, with broader implications for time-critical quantum-control and scientific-inference applications.

</details>


### [145] [Quantum impurity models: easy at equilibrium, universal in motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02130)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sergey Bravyi,Anirban Chowdhury,Arkopal Dutt,Alexandru Gheorghiu,Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究量子杂质模型的计算复杂性，证明其平衡性质可被经典高效近似，而时间演化模拟则是BQP完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型描述嵌入自由费米子大浴中的小相互作用子系统，是凝聚态与量子信息中的重要模型。作者希望厘清此类模型在经典计算与量子计算下的复杂性边界，即哪些性质可被经典算法高效求解、哪些本质上需要量子计算能力。

Method: 针对含n个费米子模式、杂质规模为常数的杂质模型哈密顿量H，利用按能标与Krylov深度组织的基底下多粒子浴激发的指数压制性质，设计经典算法计算基态与热平衡性质；并通过构造一个稳态量子处理器（其程序以自由传播费米子流的形式到达）实现通用量子计算。

Result: (1) 基态能量可在poly(n,1/ε)时间内以加性误差ε近似，优于此前最优算法的准多项式时间；(2) 在逆温度β下，Helmholtz自由能及热场双态（thermofield double state）的经典描述可在poly(n,β,1/ε)时间内以精度ε计算；(3) 对时间无关且杂质规模固定为常数的H，模拟时间演化e^{-iHt}是BQP完全的。

Conclusion: 量子杂质模型呈现鲜明对比：平衡态性质（基态能量、自由能、热场双态）可被经典算法高效近似，而时间演化则具备通用量子计算能力，是BQP完全的。

Abstract: A quantum impurity model describes a small interacting subsystem embedded into a large bath of free fermions. Here we study the computational complexity of calculating the ground energy, thermal equilibrium, and dynamical properties of these models. Our work reveals a sharp contrast: equilibrium properties can be efficiently approximated by classical means, whereas time evolution can implement a universal quantum computation. More precisely, let $H$ be the Hamiltonian of an impurity model with $n$ fermionic modes and a constant-size impurity. We show that: (1) the ground energy of $H$ can be approximated to additive error $\varepsilon$ by a classical algorithm with runtime $\textsf{poly}(n,1/\varepsilon)$, improving on the quasi-polynomial runtime of the best previously known algorithm; (2) at inverse temperature $β$, the Helmholtz free energy and a classical description of the thermofield double state can be computed to precision $\varepsilon$ in time $\textsf{poly}(n,β,1/\varepsilon)$; (3) simulating the time evolution $e^{-iHt}$ is $\textsf{BQP}$-complete, for $H$ that is time-independent and has a fixed, constant impurity size. Our algorithms exploit exponential suppression of multi-particle bath excitations in a basis organized by energy scale and Krylov depth. Our universality construction realizes a stationary quantum processor whose program arrives in a stream of freely propagating fermions.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal transducers using symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02133)
*Benoît Dubus,Julien Ladeuze,Jérémie Roland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用状态转换问题的对称群，将最优催化剂的选取化为协变形式，并证明转换器在等型分解下可块对角化，从而系统构造最优转换器，给出无结构搜索、振幅放大与振幅估计等原语的最优算法及常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转换器（transducer）框架虽与对偶敌手SDP及Las Vegas复杂度紧密对应，且可精确复合，但构造高效乃至最优转换器仍需先求解敌手SDP并显式构造酉算子，难度大。作者希望借助问题对称性简化这两步。

Method: 1）对称化论证：证明最优催化剂总可选取为在对称群表示下协变；2）证明转换器纠缠两个不同群表示，故可在Hilbert空间等型分解中选取块对角形式；3）据此针对具体算法原语构造显式最优转换器。

Result: 推导出无结构搜索、振幅放大、振幅估计等常用量子算法原语的最优转换器，且常数最优；方法将先前用表示论计算敌手下界的工作推广到最优算法的系统构造。

Conclusion: 对称群方法能同时简化敌手SDP求解与酉算子构造，为系统设计最优量子查询算法提供可行路径。

Abstract: Transducers (Belovs, Jeffery and Yolcu, 2024) are a quantum computing framework describing a quantum algorithm as a unitary converting an input state into a target state using a catalyst, an auxiliary vector that is left unchanged. They are a powerful tool in quantum algorithm design, especially in the context of quantum query complexity: feasible points of the (dual) adversary semidefinite program directly translate into transducers and the optimal transduction complexity is equal to the adversary bound, i.e. the Las Vegas complexity, which is known to characterize bounded-error quantum query complexity. Moreover, contrary to bounded-error algorithms, transducers compose exactly, which limits overheads due to controlling errors in algorithms constructed by composition. Constructing efficient, let alone optimal, transducers in terms of quantum query complexity nevertheless remains a hard task since it still requires solving the adversary SDP and constructing the unitary to obtain an explicit algorithm. In this paper, we show how using the symmetry group of state-conversion problems simplifies both steps. First, using a symmetrization argument, we prove an optimal catalyst can always be chosen covariant under a representation of the symmetry group. Second, we prove that the transducer intertwines two different representations of the group and can thus be chosen block diagonal in the isotypic decomposition of the Hilbert space. Using those methods, we then derive optimal transducers, with optimal constants, for different widely used quantum algorithmic primitives, such as unstructured search, amplitude amplification and amplitude estimation. Our approach extends previous work on the use of representation theory to compute adversary lower bounds (Høyer, Lee, and {\v S}palek, 2007; Ambainis, Magnin, Roetteler and Roland, 2011) to the systematic construction of optimal algorithms.

</details>


### [147] [A Generalized quantum Stein lemma on von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02134)
*Li Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子 Stein 引理推广到任意 von Neumann 代数上、针对凸且张量稳定的备择状态族、以 i.i.d. 正态态为零假设的情形，证明了最坏情况第二类错误指数等于正则化相对熵并具强逆性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 Stein 引理是量子假设检验的渐近理论基础，但已有结果多局限于特定代数结构（如有限维或特定类型的 von Neumann 代数）和特定备择族。作者希望建立更一般的框架，覆盖任意 von Neumann 代数、凸且张量稳定的备择族以及一般（非迹类）正态态，从而统一并扩展经典与量子 Stein 引理的适用范围。

Method: 证明综合运用了三种工具：(1) 相对熵的 hockey-stick divergence 积分表示，将相对熵分解为一系列区分度的积分；(2) 模检验界（modular testing bound），在一般 von Neumann 代数上构造可行的检验算子；(3) 凸极小极大（minimax）论证，处理凸、张量稳定备择族下的最坏情况并取正则化极限。前提假设是存在与零假设态相对熵有限的备择态。

Result: 在任意 type-I 错误容限 ε ∈ (0,1) 下，最坏情况 type-II 错误指数由正则化相对熵给出，并且满足强逆（strong converse），即该指数为精确的极限速率而非仅下界。结果对 i.i.d. 正态态与凸、张量稳定备择族在任意 von Neumann 代数上成立。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了广义量子 Stein 引理，统一并推广了有限维及已有代数情形下的 Stein 型结果，说明了正则化相对熵在最坏情况下的判别速率刻画中的核心地位，其方法（积分表示、模检验界与凸极小极大）也为一般算子代数上的量子信息论问题提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We prove a generalized quantum Stein lemma for i.i.d. normal states against convex, tensor-stable families on arbitrary von Neumann algebras. Assuming the existence of an alternative state with finite relative entropy from the null state, we show that, at every type-I error tolerance $\varepsilon\in (0,1)$, the worst case type-II error exponent is achieved with the regularized relative entropy with a strong converse. The proof combines the integral representation of relative entropy by hockey-stick divergences, a modular testing bound, and convex minimax argument.

</details>


### [148] [Random Quantum Circuits Beyond Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02135)
*Shih-Han Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机量子电路（近似酉设计）在单个输出概率分布层面的定量保证：强ε-近似酉k设计对每个输出概率的分布与Porter-Thomas分布（Beta(1, 2^n-1)）的Kolmogorov距离为O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n))) + ε)，且该界在常数因子意义下最优；局部不变性可将误差指数级改善至2^{-Ω(k)}级别，并给出全变差距离保证，进而导出输出分布Shannon熵的偏差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路旨在高效复现理想随机量子演化的统计性质，而近似酉设计通过匹配Haar随机酉算子的各阶矩来逼近这一目标。已有工作关注矩匹配的误差，但设计在多大程度上能复现单个输出概率的完整分布尚缺乏定量刻画。本文动机即填补这一空白：确定矩匹配所能保证的分布精度，并识别能显著提升精度的额外结构。

Method: 以强ε-近似酉k设计为对象，采用Kolmogorov距离与全变差距离作为度量工具，将每个输出概率的分布与有限维Porter-Thomas分布（参数为1和2^n-1的Beta分布）进行对比。通过分析矩匹配阶数k与原子维度2^n之间的关系推导误差上界，并证明其在常数因子意义下的最优性。进一步引入作用于log k + O(1)个量子比特上的酉平移不变性，分析局部不变性对k依赖性的影响。

Result: 对任意n量子比特上的强ε-近似酉k设计，每个输出概率的分布与Porter-Thomas分布的Kolmogorov距离不超过O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n))) + ε)，且该界最优（至常数因子）。若设计在log k + O(1)个量子比特的酉平移下不变，则误差降为2^{-Ω(k)} + O(ε)（Kolmogorov距离）及2^{-Ω(k)} + O(ε log(2/ε))（全变差距离），即对k的依赖获得指数级改善。作为应用，若强近似设计各输出概率的边缘分布与Haar对应物Kolmogorov距离为η，则输出分布的期望Shannon熵与Haar值之差为O(√η)。

Conclusion: 本文明确了矩匹配所保证的分布精度：仅靠匹配矩，输出概率分布误差存在最优的、不可进一步均匀改进的下界；而局部不变性这一额外结构可带来指数级提升，并覆盖更强的全变差度量。结果同时刻画了精度保证与提升精度所需的结构，并将分布精度与Shannon熵等物理量联系起来。

Abstract: Random quantum circuits aim to efficiently reproduce the statistical properties of ideal random quantum evolution. One approach is to construct approximate unitary designs, which match the moments of Haar-random unitaries up to a prescribed order with controlled error. In this work, we establish quantitative guarantees for how accurately these designs reproduce the full distributions of individual output probabilities. We show that, for every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design on $n$ qubits, the distribution of each individual output probability is within $O\left(\sqrt{\frac{2^n}{k(k+2^n)}}+\varepsilon\right)$ in Kolmogorov distance of the finite-dimensional Porter-Thomas distribution, a beta distribution with parameters $1$ and $2^n-1$. This bound is optimal up to constant factors, implying that a substantially better uniform bound requires additional structure. We further show that local invariance yields an exponential improvement in the dependence on $k$, with guarantees also in the stronger metric of total variation: every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design invariant under unitary translations acting on $\log k+O(1)$ qubits achieves error $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$ in Kolmogorov distance and $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$ in total variation distance. These results identify both the distributional accuracy guaranteed by moment matching and the additional structure that substantially improves it. As an application, we show that if the marginal distribution of each output probability of a strong approximate design is within Kolmogorov distance $η$ of its Haar counterpart, then the expected Shannon entropy of the output distribution differs from its Haar value by $O(\sqrtη)$.

</details>


### [149] [One-Shot any Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02137)
*Andrew C. Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了任意单次测量码具有自纠错性质，并给出将任意[[n,k]] CSS量子低密度奇偶校验码变换为[[nm,k]]单次纠错码的构造方法，该码具有高效局部解码器和恒定阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决量子LDPC码在单次纠错（single-shot）框架下的构造与解码效率问题，并探索如何在不损失阈值的前提下增强误差抑制能力。

Method: 首先给出任意单次码自纠错的简证；随后通过将输入CSS QLDPC码扩展为[[nm,k]]码，构造出支持单次量子纠错的码，并设计运行时间为O(log m)并行时间的局部解码器。

Result: 在联合局部随机物理与测量噪声下，存在恒定阈值p_RG>0，T轮纠错后的逻辑失败概率为O(Tn)exp[-Ω(m^α)]；若输入码具有阈值p_c和误差抑制Ω(n^β)，则输出码将误差抑制增强至Ω(m^α n^β)，并保持解码器独立阈值p_RG，当p_c<p_RG时实现增强。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了单次纠错码的通用变换框架，实现了高效局部解码与恒定阈值，并给出了误差抑制增强的充分条件，为构建高性能量子LDPC码提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We first provide a simple proof showing that any single-shot code is self-correcting. We then show that any $[[n,k]]$ CSS quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) code can be transformed into an $[[nm,k]]$ CSS QLDPC code with single-shot (SS) quantum error correction and an efficient local decoder which runs in $O(\log m)$ parallel time. Below a constant threshold $p_{\rm RG}>0$ for joint local stochastic physical and measurement noise, the logical failure probability over $T$ correction rounds is bounded by \begin{equation*}
  O(T n)\exp[-Ω(m^α)] \end{equation*} for a constant $α>0$; we say that the \textit{threshold} is $p_{\rm RG}$ and \textit{error suppression} is $Ω(m^α)$. Moreover, if the input code is equipped with a single-shot decoder with threshold $p_c>0$ and error suppression $Ω(n^β)$, then the output code \textit{enhances} the error suppression to $Ω(m^α n^β)$, while maintaining the decoder-independent threshold $p_{\rm RG}$, and thus \textit{enhanced} if $p_c < p_{\rm RG}$.

</details>


### [150] [A provable quantum advantage for approximate optimization via decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02145)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Elies Gil-Fuster,Benjamin D. M. Jones,Jens Eisert,Franz J. Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在预言机模型下证明了DQI（解码量子干涉）算法相对所有多项式时间经典算法在近似优化任务folded OPI上具有严格优势，并通过改进算法进一步扩大了该量子-经典差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI是一种具有强性能保证、利用优化与编码理论对偶性的量子近似优化框架，但其核心问题——能否可证明地超越所有多项式时间经典算法——此前尚未解决。本文旨在确立这种可证明的优势。

Method: 考虑folded OPI（folded optimal polynomial intersection）优化任务，其接受集随机选取并通过成员预言机访问。证明建立在Jordan等人的DQI近似优化框架之上，并将Yamakawa-Zhandry精确搜索预言机分离背后的经典下界方法扩展到近似情形；同时基于Sun-Wootters、Horinaga-Yamakawa及Jo的近期工作，构造了DQI算法的改进版本。

Result: 在多项式时间经典算法可达近似比与DQI算法可达近似比之间建立了严格差距。改进版DQI算法在folded OPI上取得更大差距。以码率0.3为例，DQI与改进算法的期望得分约为0.85和0.95；而经典算法要在采样实例上以常数概率将0.65的经典阈值超出任意固定量，需要超多项式数量的经典成员查询。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在预言机模型下给出了DQI相对所有多项式时间经典算法的可证明优势，并通过改进算法强化了量子分离，为DQI范式提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a novel paradigm for tackling approximate optimization problems on quantum computers. This framework comes with strong performance guarantees and exploits a well-established duality between optimization and coding theory. A central question, however, is whether DQI can actually provably outperform all polynomial-time classical algorithms. In this work, we establish such an advantage in an oracle setting: we consider an optimization task called folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI), where the acceptance sets are chosen randomly and accessed through membership oracles. We establish a strict gap between the approximation ratio achievable by any polynomial-time classical algorithm and the approximation ratio achieved by the DQI algorithm. Our proof builds on Jordan et al.'s DQI framework for approximate optimization and extends the classical lower-bound method underlying Yamakawa and Zhandry's exact-search oracle separation to approximation. Building on recent developments by Sun and Wootters, Horinaga and Yamakawa, and Jo, we further show that a modified version of the DQI algorithm achieves a strictly larger gap on the folded OPI problem, yielding an even stronger quantum separation. As a concrete example, for code rate $0.3$, DQI and the modified algorithm achieve expected scores of approximately $0.85$ and $0.95$, respectively. In contrast, exceeding the classical threshold of $0.65$ by any fixed amount with constant probability on sampled instances requires super-polynomially many classical membership queries.

</details>


### [151] [Polynomial-time additive-error estimation of output probabilities for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02146)
*Matthew Coudron,Michael J. Gullans,Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Shi Jie Samuel Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文给出了一个确定性经典算法，可在 poly(n,1/ε) 时间内估计常数深度量子电路输出概率 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|²，达到加性误差 ε，显著改进了此前最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对常数深度量子电路输出概率的经典估计，即使电路深度为常数，最优算法仍需 n^{O(log n)} 时间（一般连接）、n^{O(log log n)}（几何局域）以及 n^{O(1)}（2D 几何局域），效率仍有较大提升空间，作者希望找到更快的经典模拟/估计方法。

Method: 提出一个确定性经典算法，用于估计 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|²，其中 U 为常数深度、有界扇入门、任意连接的量子电路，x 为任意 n 比特输出串，达到加性误差 ε。

Result: 算法运行时间为 poly(n, 1/ε)，优于此前一般连接情形的 n^{O(log n)}、几何局域情形的 n^{O(log log n)}，以及 2D 几何局域情形的 n^{O(1)}。

Conclusion: 该工作将常数深度量子电路输出概率的经典估计从拟多项式时间降到多项式时间，在任意连接下也实现了 poly(n,1/ε) 复杂度，推进了经典模拟常数深度量子电路的相关理论边界。

Abstract: We give a deterministic classical algorithm that estimates $|\langle x|U|0^n\rangle|^2$ to additive error $\varepsilon$ in $\mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$ time, where $U$ is a constant-depth quantum circuit comprised of gates with bounded fan-in and arbitrary connectivity, and $x$ is an arbitrary $n$-bit output string. This improves over prior state-of-the-art algorithms that takes $n^{O(log(n))}$ time for the same task, $n^{O(log(log(n))}$ when $U$ is geometrically local, and $n^{O(1)}$ for 2D geometrically-local circuits.

</details>


### [152] [The Robustness of QAC0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02154)
*Daniel Grier,Jackson Morris,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究QAC^0电路对误差容忍和门集合修改的鲁棒性：证明其内在误差可通过精确振幅放大完全消除，且其计算能力可仅由广义Toffoli、S和Hadamard门近似实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAC^0通常允许非零误差，且允许任意单量子比特门。作者希望探究这两个假设是否必要：误差是否可消除，以及计算能力是否依赖于任意单量子比特门的使用。

Method: 1) 对并行W-test的误差，采用多副本情形下精确振幅放大的新应用来消除；2) 对门集合限制，构造由广义Toffoli、S、Hadamard组成的QAC^0电路，从原电路的经典描述出发高效近似实现原电路。

Result: 精确QAC^0（记为EQAC^0）能用多项式多个经典输入副本精确模拟TC^0；对每个固定素数p，EQAC^0可计算AC^0[p]之外的全布尔函数；任意QAC^0电路可由仅含广义Toffoli、S、Hadamard的QAC^0电路近似实现，且该近似电路可高效构造。

Conclusion: QAC^0对误差和门集合限制均具有鲁棒性：其非零误差可完全消除，且其计算能力不依赖于任意单量子比特门，仅需有限门集合即可近似实现。

Abstract: In this work we study the robustness of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ with respect to error tolerance and modifications to its gate-set. First, we investigate whether the non-zero error typically allowed for $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits computing Boolean functions is truly necessary. We show that the error inherent in the parallel $W$-test of \cite{grier_morris_wu} can be eliminated entirely via a novel application of exact amplitude amplification in the many-copies context. Consequently, we find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ can \textit{exactly} simulate $\mathsf{TC}^0$ with polynomially many copies of the classical input and that for every fixed prime $p$ exact $\mathsf{QAC}^0$, $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$, can compute total Boolean functions outside of $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$.
  Second, we ask to what extent the computational power of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ follows from the fact that arbitrary single-qubit gates may be used at any point in the circuit. We find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is in fact robust to restrictions on which single-qubit gates are permitted: every $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit can be approximately implemented by a $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit consisting of just generalized Toffoli, $S$, and Hadamard gates. Moreover, this approximating circuit can be constructed efficiently from a classical description of the original circuit.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在仅有单个可测量子比特和连通的参考骨干结构下，利用量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门实现相干态转移，可在无需校准中间链路哈密顿量参数的前提下，以海森堡极限学习N量子比特有界度两体哈密顿量的全部O(N)个独立参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习协议通常假设能够对**整个系统**进行直接测量，而在实际场景中往往只有有限的测量接口。本文旨在放宽这一假设，探究仅凭单个可测量量子比特能否完成对大规模多体哈密顿量全部参数的完整学习。

Method: 核心手段是借助快速单比特控制与连通的参考骨干（backbone），通过**量子信号处理（QSP）**合成鲁棒SWAP门，将远离可测比特的、受相应哈密顿量参数演化影响的量子态相干地转移到可测比特上；该转移过程无需预先校准中间链路的哈密顿量参数。同时采用并行学习架构。

Result: 在N量子比特链上，协议实现总查询时间$\widetilde{O}(N)$，并保持海森堡极限的精度标度；这些标度在链结构上（在对数因子范围内）与基本的精度下界和信息传播下界相匹配。该框架可进一步推广至任意有界度相互作用图。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅通过单一的局部测量接口即可学习数量可观（extensive）的多体哈密顿量参数，为可扩展的哈密顿量学习提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [154] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子模拟中的 stabilizer rank 问题推广到高斯情形，在玻色与费米系统中证明：任何纯非高斯态的张量幂，其近似边界高斯秩随模数指数增长，从而表明非高斯性普遍导致指数级高斯分解复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相干分解为经典可处理自由态的模拟范式，其复杂度由分解秩决定。证明该秩的强下界极为困难（如 qubit stabilizer rank 问题）。作者希望在高斯（玻色/费米）版本中建立稳健的指数下界，以刻画基于分解的经典模拟的根本极限。

Method: 对费米子情形，将问题归约到四个模，并结合 Majorana 谱界；对玻色子情形，采用高斯后选择与基于熵的秩界。技术核心是在张量幂下分析近似边界高斯秩的增长。

Result: 证明了：对任意有限模上的纯非高斯态（费米情形需具确定宇称），当固定范数误差小于 1 时，其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩至少指数增长。具体给出四模费米 GHZ 态与玻色单光子态的显式指数下界；玻色结果不依赖目标态能量假设。

Conclusion: 非高斯性普遍蕴含着指数级高斯分解复杂度，为基于分解的玻色与费米系统经典模拟设定了根本性限制。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [155] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明完全置换不变性限制了关联跨信道使用带来的量子通信速率优势：优化相干信息/单次使用收敛到单次使用最大值，故渐近优化可归约为单次使用；同时给出纯源纠缠生成的强逆定理，并揭示完全有界范数与夹心Rényi信道量共享变分结构，说明置换协变可统一处理可乘性与可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨信道使用的关联可提升量子通信速率，但这也使得确定量子容量需在任意大块上优化，成为核心困难。作者希望刻画在何种结构约束下这一优势受限，从而简化量子容量的渐近刻画，并统一若干已知的可乘性/可加性结果。

Method: 对任意有限维无记忆信道，施加完全置换不变（置换协变）输入；利用De Finetti约化与置换协变，分析优化后的每使用相干信息、纯源纠缠生成的保真度、以及完全有界范数与夹心Rényi信道量的变分表达式。

Result: 置换不变下的优化每使用相干信息收敛到单次使用最大值，并在有限块长处达到其上确界；有限块超可加性仍可能，而渐近优化归约为单次信道使用。对置换不变输入的纯源纠缠生成给出强逆：任何高于单次相干信息最大值的固定速率，保真度趋于零。此外，完全有界范数与夹心Rényi信道量在共享变分框架下获得其已知可乘性与可加性的系统替代证明；对置换不变输入，输出每使用熵的渐近下降不会低于单次最小值（对Rényi阶>1及von Neumann熵成立）。

Conclusion: 置换对称性把跨使用关联的优势限制在有限块，使渐近量子通信优化退化为单次使用问题，并将对称性的作用从通信界扩展到输出熵控制；同时为多个信道量的可乘性/可加性提供统一变分证明。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [156] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从 Gibbs 态副本中学习稠密 SYK Hamiltonian 的问题，利用模型的随机平均场结构克服现有方法对几何局域性或有限交互度的依赖，证明在任意常数温度下可多项式样本高效学习整个 Hamiltonian 至逆多项式精度，并在低温小常数情形给出准多项式时间学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Hamiltonian 学习算法通常依赖几何局域性或交互度有界，而 SYK 模型中每个四体交互与 Θ(n^3) 个其他项重叠，导致这些方法失效。作者希望寻找新的结构性质以突破该障碍。

Method: 作者利用 SYK 模型的随机平均场结构，在任意常数温度下分析随机耦合的高概率性质，并设计样本高效的学习算法；此外，在逆温度限制为足够小的常数时，构造了准多项式时间学习算法。

Result: 在任意常数温度下，以高概率（对 SYK 耦合随机性）可用多项式数量的样本将整个 Hamiltonian 学习至逆多项式精度；在逆温度为足够小常数时，得到一个与样本高效算法定性不同的准多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: SYK Hamiltonian 的学习障碍可通过随机平均场结构被克服，稠密相互作用的 Hamiltonian 也能实现样本高效学习，且在不同温度区域存在不同性质的学习算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [157] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究被全局酉算符"混淆"的非相互作用哈密顿量学习问题，证明其DQC1-hard，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料建模（如平均场理论、格林函数、密度泛函理论）中，准粒子谱是下游计算的核心。此设定将非相互作用结构隐藏在未知全局酉算符之下，契合材料模拟需求，且目标是恢复能级权重而不必重建酉算符。

Method: 证明该学习问题为DQC1-hard；提出SPICES方法，将Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理结合，利用单粒子能量与复逆温度下配分函数零点所对应的傅里叶频率之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明问题具有DQC1-hard复杂性；在若干自然假设下，SPICES能在Wasserstein距离意义下高效恢复谱分布。

Conclusion: 由于准粒子谱在材料计算中普遍存在且SPICES简单实用，该方法有望成为量子材料模拟应用的有前景路线。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Hyperbolic lattices with mass disorder: Phases and phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02192)
*Sheersh Sen,Christopher A. Leong,Bitan Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究十边形、八边形双曲晶格上的最近邻紧束缚模型，通过核多项式方法计算有无质量无序时的态密度，揭示狄拉克液体、费米液体与平带三种零能行为及无序驱动的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲晶格具有负曲率与独特的配位结构，其低能电子态可能呈现与欧氏晶格不同的量子行为；同时质量无序对这类格点上电子态及态密度的影响尚不清楚，作者希望探讨其相变规律与临界性质。

Method: 在庞加莱圆盘上构建以面心为中心的{10,3}、{8,3}、{8,4}双曲晶格（Schläfli符号），采用开边界条件下的最近邻紧束缚模型，利用核多项式方法大规模数值计算平均态密度与典型态密度，引入质量无序并分析其相变行为与临界指数。

Result: {10,3}、{8,3}、{8,4}晶格在零能（带中心）附近的态密度分别表现为趋零、有限与发散，对应狄拉克液体、费米液体与平带。双曲狄拉克液体对弱质量无序稳定，中等无序下发生半金属-金属量子相变，更强无序下发生安德森金属-绝缘体相变；另两个系统仅表现出后者。各相变的临界指数均接近在位势无序所驱动的相变临界指数。

Conclusion: 质量无序可驱动双曲晶格体系发生多种量子相变，其中狄拉克液体存在半金属-金属-绝缘体的双阶段转变，而费米液体与平带体系仅发生金属-绝缘体转变；临界指数与在位势无序情形相近，表明双曲格点上无序驱动的相变具有一定的普适性。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor (NN) tight-binding models (TBMs) on plaquette-centered $\{ 10,3\}$ (Schläfli symbol), $\{ 8,3\}$, and $\{ 8,4\}$ hyperbolic lattices on a Poincaré disk with open boundary conditions display a vanishing, a finite, and a diverging density of states (DOS) near zero energy (band center), respectively, yielding a Dirac liquid, a Fermi liquid, and a flat band. Emergent bipartite nature of these lattices within the framework of NN-TBMs, allows us to scrutinize the impact of mass disorder on the electronic states and DOS therein. From extensive numerical calculations of the average and typical DOS using the kernel polynomial method, we show that hyperbolic Dirac liquid remains stable against weak mass disorder, undergoing a semimetal-to-metal quantum phase transition at moderate disorder, followed by an Anderson metal-to-insulator transition at even stronger disorder. The remaining two systems display only the latter transition. Critical exponents near all these transitions are found to be close to the ones mediated by on-site potential disorder.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Migration Flows: Population Scaling and Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00192)
*Cook Hyun Kim,Sangjoon Park,Seoyoung Jin,Jaiyong Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过县际迁移机制解释城际平均流量的人口标度律与重尾净流量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示城际平均流量随城市人口标度以及城际净流量呈重尾分布这一现象的微观机制。

Method: 假设县对净流量服从高斯分布，标准差正比于其平均有向流量；在固定相关系数下县对净流量之和为高斯，并将该条件高斯密度对相关分布求平均，同时提出生成该混合方差的随机模型。

Result: 密度依赖的出发与到达活动决定平均流量随出发地和目的地城市人口的标度；对相关分布取平均可产生带高斯截断的中间幂律。

Conclusion: 县际迁移机制能够同时解释城际平均流量的标度律与城际净流量分布的重尾特征。

Abstract: We show how migration between counties in different cities can explain both the population scaling of mean intercity flows and heavy-tailed intercity net-flow distributions. Density-dependent departure and arrival activities determine how mean flows scale with the populations of the origin and destination cities. We assume Gaussian county-pair net flows with standard deviations proportional to their mean directional flows. At a fixed correlation between county-pair net flows, their sum is Gaussian. Averaging this conditional Gaussian density over the correlation distribution can produce an intermediate power law with a Gaussian cutoff. We also present a stochastic model that generates the variance distribution used in this mixture.

</details>


### [160] [Active-Matter Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00428)
*Yun-Qian Lin,Rui-Han Liu,Yu-Han Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出从活性物质中提取能量的极简电池模型，并解析给出充放电特性；引入维持活性所需能量成本后，充放电功率-效率权衡呈环状，效率存在有限时间极大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用活性物质驱动功能器件日益受到关注，但缺乏描述从活性物质中提取并储存能量的最小理论模型及其充放电性能刻画。

Method: 构建极简电池模型，用于储存从活性物质中提取的能量，并解析推导其充电与放电特性；模型中纳入维持活性的能量成本。

Result: 当考虑维持活性的能量成本时，充电功率-效率权衡关系呈环状（loop-like），效率出现有限时间极大值，与被动能量器件形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 活性物质能量储存具有不同于被动器件的独特热力学与动力学特征，所提极简模型为理解并优化活性物质驱动的功能器件提供了解析框架。

Abstract: Harnessing active matter to power functional devices has attracted growing interest. We propose a minimal battery model for storing energy extracted from active matter and derive its charging and discharging characteristics analytically. When the energetic cost of sustaining activity is included, the charging power-efficiency trade-off becomes loop-like and the efficiency attains a finite-time maximum, in sharp contrast to passive energy devices.

</details>


### [161] [Effects of on-site Gaussian disorder in the ferromagnetic Blume Capel model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01090)
*Kimberly J. Silverman,Daria Lhommedieu,Alykhan Rajan,Kathryn A. Cooper,Richard T. Scalettar,Eduardo Ibarra-García-Padilla*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡罗模拟研究了在位高斯无序（位置依赖晶场）对Blume-Capel模型相变行为的影响：无序降低二阶相变线温度$T_c$，但$T_c$在大无序宽度下仍保持有限；同时无序显著抑制并最终消除三临界点附近的一阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Blume-Capel模型中晶场$D$是均匀的，而实际材料中局域空位密度控制的晶场可能存在空间涨落（无序）。作者希望探究在位无序如何改变相图结构，特别是二阶相变线、三临界点及一阶相变的存在性。

Method: 采用Markov Chain Monte Carlo方法，引入位置依赖晶场$D_i$，服从高斯分布$P(D_i)\sim\exp[-(D_i-\bar{D})^2/2\sigma^2]$，在固定$\bar{D}$下计算相变温度$T_c$随无序宽度$\sigma$的变化，并考察对三临界点的影响。

Result: 在位高斯无序使二阶相变线整体向低温移动，$T_c$随$\sigma$增大而降低；结果显示即便在较大$\sigma$下$T_c$仍保持有限。对于常规均匀Blume-Capel模型的三临界点，无序显著压缩一阶相变区域，并在大$\sigma$时将其完全消除。

Conclusion: 位置依赖的晶场无序不会破坏二阶相变线的存在性（$T_c$保持有限），但会强烈抑制一阶相变，最终消除三临界点。这表明无序是调控Blume-Capel类系统相图、尤其是三临界行为的重要参数。

Abstract: We conduct a numerical study of a generalization of the Blume-Capel model in which the crystal field $D_i$, which controls the local vacancy density, is site dependent rather than uniform. We employ Markov Chain Monte Carlo to compute the shifted phase transition line $T_c$ as a function of the width $σ$ of a gaussian distribution $P(D_i) \sim \exp( -(D_i - \bar{D})^2/2 σ^2)$ at fixed $\bar{D}$. We characterize how the presence of this on-site Gaussian disorder reduces $T_c$ across the second order transition line, and discuss how our results suggest that $T_c$ remains finite even at large values of $σ$. We also determine the effects of disorder on the tricritical point of the conventional, uniform, Blume-Capel model, finding that it significantly suppresses the first-order phase transition, eliminating it entirely at large values of $σ$.

</details>


### [162] [Generative Modeling of Stochastic Dynamics for Long-Time Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00546)
*Yang-yang Tan,Jinyang Li,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 短时构型对的生成式扩散模型可学习有限时间转移核，迭代后能预测远离训练滞后的非平衡长时动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡随机动力学的精确随机方程通常难以获得，需要一种无需运动方程即可预测长时演化的方法。

Method: 利用固定短时间滞后下的构型对训练生成式扩散模型，学习有限时间转移核，并通过迭代该核将动力学传播到远大于训练滞后的时间尺度。

Result: 在二维Model B中复现了动态临界标度和自相似t^{1/3}粗化，且在训练尺寸两倍的格点和未参与训练的初始系综上仍与直接模拟一致；在周期光势场驱动胶体体系中，用10分钟轨迹预测随后20分钟的粒子流和平均通过时间，精度在实验不确定度内。

Conclusion: 短时观测包含预测长时涌现非平衡动力学所需的信息。

Abstract: Exact stochastic equations for non-equilibrium dynamics are rarely accessible. We show that the long-time evolution of stochastic dynamics can be predicted from configuration pairs at a fixed short time lag, without knowledge of the equation of motion. Generative diffusion models learn the finite-time transition kernel from these pairs, and iterating it propagates the dynamics far beyond the training lag. For two-dimensional Model B, the diffusive dynamics of a conserved order parameter, the learned kernels reproduce dynamic critical scaling and self-similar $t^{1/3}$ coarsening. Agreement with direct simulations persists on lattices twice the largest training size and for initial ensembles absent from training. For driven colloids in a periodic optical potential, ten minutes of measured trajectories suffice to predict the particle current and mean passage time over the next twenty minutes within experimental uncertainty. Short-time observations thus contain the information needed to predict emergent non-equilibrium dynamics at much longer times.

</details>


### [163] [The Ericson Transition in Time-Reversal Invariant Systems: Symplectically Invariant Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00624)
*Simon Köhnes,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在Heidelberg方法框架下，研究奇数自旋、时间反演不变系统在Ericson区间的非对角散射矩阵元与截面分布，证明其领头项为高斯与指数行为，并推导次领头项与数值模拟对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间反演不变且奇数自旋的混沌散射系统中，Ericson区间内非对角散射矩阵元及截面涨落的统计分布规律，以检验普适性并刻画有限尺寸修正。

Method: 采用普适Heidelberg方法（基于随机矩阵理论与反应理论的统计散射理论），对相关分布作渐近展开，分别求出领头阶与次领头阶项，并将理论结果与数值模拟进行比对。

Result: 证明了该分布领头项呈高斯行为（散射矩阵元）与指数行为（截面），并给出了次领头阶修正项；数值模拟与含次领头项的理论结果相符。

Conclusion: 在奇数自旋、时间反演不变的Ericson区间，非对角散射量的分布具有普适的高斯/指数领头行为，次领头修正可定量描述有限尺寸效应。

Abstract: We investigate the distributions of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross-sections in the Ericson regime for time-reversal invariant systems with odd spin. Within the universal Heidelberg approach we prove that Gaussian and exponential behavior is found as the leading-order term of an asymptotic expansion of the aforementioned distributions. Furthermore, the subleading-order term is derived and compared with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [164] [CTMRG as a tensor-network message-passing algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00631)
*Illia Lukin,Andrii Sotnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将角转移矩阵方程及基于其上的角转移矩阵重整化群，重新表述为定义在一般平面图上的局部方程；这些方程仅依赖平面图的局部子图，并通过一致粘合描述整图。求解可得消息张量与角矩阵，用于为任意连通子图构造MPS环境。作者在铁磁Ising模型、多种无限晶格（含双曲晶格）及有限图上进行测试，结果相对信念传播有系统性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角转移矩阵与CTMRG传统上多用于规则二维晶格，缺乏对一般平面图的统一、局部化表述；同时希望在超越信念传播的近似方法上获得更精确的张量网络环境构造手段。

Method: 将角转移矩阵方程与CTMRG表述为一般平面图上的局部方程，仅依赖局部子图并一致粘合；求解得到消息张量与角矩阵，并据此为任意连通子图构造MPS环境。

Result: 在铁磁Ising模型、多种无限晶格（包括双曲晶格）以及有限图上进行测试，方法相对信念传播表现出系统性改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的局部化CTMRG框架可推广至一般平面图与有限图，并能构造通用连通子图的MPS环境，在测试中稳定优于信念传播。

Abstract: We formulate the corner transfer matrix equations, and the corner transfer matrix renormalization group built on them, as a set of local equations on general planar graphs. These equations depend only on the local subgraphs of the planar graph and are glued together consistently to describe the whole graph. Solving them yields message tensors and corner matrices, which can be used to construct an MPS environment for any connected subgraph. We test the proposed equations on the ferromagnetic Ising model, on a variety of infinite lattices (including hyperbolic ones) and on finite graphs, and find systematic improvement over belief propagation.

</details>


### [165] [Thermal entanglement transitions from strong $\textrm{SU}(2)$ symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00826)
*Siqi Mo,Ehud Altman,Samuel J. Garratt*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究强SU(2)对称铁磁性自旋系统Gibbs态中的混合态纠缠，证明有限温度有序相变与纠缠相变相关联，并给出顺磁相与铁磁相中纠缠度量随系统尺寸的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强对称Lindblad方程的稳态可展现纠缠相变，但有限温度有序相变与纠缠相变之间的关联此前尚不明确，需要建立混合态纠缠度量与自旋关联之间的关系。

Method: 首先建立混合态纠缠度量（可提纯纠缠、对数纠缠负度）与全局SU(2)单态下自旋关联的关系；随后提出SU(2)单态热态的半经典理论，在大S极限下将全局单态约束简化为对总磁化的高斯压制，并导出描述单态扇区自旋关联的场论；结合三维格点Monte Carlo模拟与一维自旋1/2精确数值验证。

Result: 顺磁相中两半系统的可提纯纠缠与对数纠缠负度随自旋数平方根对数增长；铁磁相中这些量随系统尺寸增长显著更快。半经典理论在场论层面刻画了低温和高温下的混合态纠缠探针行为，数值结果支持解析预测。

Conclusion: 有限温度有序相变伴随纠缠相变，强对称Lindblad方程稳态可呈现纠缠相变；所建立的半经典场论为研究混合态纠缠标度提供了有效工具，并得到数值模拟验证。

Abstract: We study mixed-state entanglement in Gibbs states with strong $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ symmetry, focusing on locally interacting spin systems with ferromagnetic interactions. Our main result is to show that finite-temperature ordering transitions are associated with entanglement transitions, and therefore the steady state of strongly symmetric Lindbladians can exhibit entanglement transitions. While in the paramagnetic phase it is known that the distillable entanglement and logarithmic entanglement negativity between two halves of a large system grow logarithmically with the square root of the number of spins, we show that in ferromagnetic phases these quantities grow parametrically faster with system size. To arrive at this result we first establish relations between these mixed-state entanglement measures and spin correlations in states that are singlets under global $\rm{SU}(2)$ symmetry transformations. We then introduce a semiclassical theory for $\rm{SU}(2)$ singlet thermal states. While the global singlet constraint generally enters this theory as a complicated function of the full semiclassical spin configuration, we show that in large $S$ limit it simplifies drastically to a Gaussian suppression of total magnetization in disordered phases as well as in ordered phases in the vicinity of continuous thermal phase transitions. This leads us to a field theory describing spin correlations in the singlet sector. Using this we determine the behavior of various probes of mixed-state entanglement at low and at high temperatures, supporting our analytical results using numerical Monte Carlo simulations of a three-dimensional lattice realization of our semiclassical theory. We also use exact numerics in one-dimensional spin-$1/2$ systems to confirm our predictions for the scaling of mixed-state entanglement with correlation length and system size in the paramagnetic phase.

</details>


### [166] [Dynamical Critical Properties of the Random-Bond three-state Potts Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01176)
*Zeynep Demir Vatansever,Ulvi Kanbur,Mohammed Sadra Najafi,Muktish Acharyya,Erol Vatansever*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟研究二维随机键三态Potts模型的动态临界行为，发现键无序显著削弱临界慢化但保持静态普适类不变，并观察到缺乏自平均性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究键无序（bond randomness）如何影响二维三态Potts模型的动态临界行为，特别是临界慢化是否被削弱，以及无序是否改变其静态普适类，这一问题对理解无序系统临界动力学具有重要意义。

Method: 采用Wolff和Swendsen-Wang集团（cluster）算法进行大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，在与无序相关的临界温度下计算积分与指数自关联时间，并通过有限尺寸标度提取动态临界指数z；同时利用Wolff集团尺寸的有限尺寸标度获得γ/ν。

Result: 动态临界指数z从纯系统的约0.54降至强无序时的约0.24，表明临界慢化被显著削弱；得到γ/ν≈1.73，与二维三态Potts模型普适类一致；比热和自关联时间等静态与动态可观测量均表现出缺乏自平均性（lack of self-averaging）。

Conclusion: 键无序强烈影响临界动力学（显著降低z、削弱临界慢化），但不改变底层的静态普适类；同时静态和动态可观测量均缺乏自平均性。

Abstract: We study the dynamic critical behavior of the two-dimensional random-bond three-state Potts model using large-scale Monte Carlo simulations with the Wolff and Swendsen--Wang cluster algorithms. At the disorder-dependent critical temperature, we compute integrated and exponential autocorrelation times and extract the dynamic critical exponent $z$ via finite-size scaling.
